{"title":"偷星者与失忆公爵：全词汇高复现·英语一阅读趣读版","expectedEntryCount":9365,"storageKey":"vocabbook-9765e1db1c3a-","chapters":[{"id":"v01c01","title":"The Silver Flower","volume":"卷一｜假婚约与失踪证人","anchor":"chapter-0001","paragraphs":["Mara Vale arrived at the auction wearing a red blouse that belonged to her landlady and a necklace that belonged, temporarily, to a woman she disliked. The necklace was an accessory to the costume. The blouse was an act of faith. Its owner had threatened to divorce her husband if he let Mara leave the household looking like a wounded pigeon.","He had offered his overcoat instead. In its pocket Mara found a football ticket, a pencil, and a peppermint sweet whose absorption of lint was almost complete. She left the ticket, kept the pencil, and ate the sweet. Theft required a reasonable baseline of energy.","The banqueting room smelled of spicy food, expensive shampoo, and money that had never needed to explain itself. Beyond its doors, an affluent audience admired a mechanical silver flower beneath a glass dome. Mara had crossed the city to remove it. Her mother had made its smallest petal; Mara knew the particular curve as well as she knew her own name.","A catalogue card called it a prize-winning European ornament. Its acquisition history began with a dead collector and ended with the Ardent Foundation. Between those points lay an alarming absence. Elena Vale had disappeared from that history entirely.","Mara read the biographical note twice. A former mentor had once taught her to differentiate a bad repair from an intentional concealment. This was the second sort. The silver chain, supposedly an original fitting, covered a screw that should have been visible. Someone had changed the flower since her mother died.","\"Admiring the workmanship?\" asked a young man beside her.","\"The competence of the liar who wrote this.\"","His delighted expression suggested that he was an optimist about interpersonal disaster. \"I am the liar's ex-husband. We may prosper together.\"","\"Then I ought to charge admission.\"","He introduced himself as Rowan Ash, a banker by family obligation and a non-celebrity by repeated public failure. Mara had seen his face in enough advertisements to cast doubt on the latter claim. He noticed her looking at his silk collar and lifted the brim of an imaginary hat.","\"I try to radiate humility. Unfortunately, the jewelry interferes.\"","\"Try thrift.\"","\"My grandparent tried it once. The family still commemorates the occasion at Christmas.\"","Mara laughed despite herself. While Rowan described a drawn-out argument over which branch of his family had paid to upholster the chairs, she watched the staff. One attendant carried an auxiliary key clipped to an elastic strap. Another checked identification at the service door. Neither looked underneath the flower's table.","She needed a disturbance lasting roughly twenty seconds. Rowan, whose disposition seemed unusually generous towards trouble, might be capable of providing one.","\"Would you be willing to do something impractical?\" she asked.","\"Darling, you must refine the parameter.\"","\"Ask whether the chairs are made of genuine nineteenth-century linen. Demand transparency.\"","He considered the nearest chair with fresh indignation. \"A question of sustainability. Splendid.\"","He crossed the floor. Mara slipped into a closet holding spare clothes, a dirty sweater, and a trunk of ceremonial costume. An abandoned sandal propped open the bottom drawer. She could not hold onto the necklace and crawl through the service hatch, so she hid it in a locker and left a discreet note: Borrowed for restoration. Please refrain from arrest until Monday.","She had no intention of keeping it. There was vanity, and there was paperwork.","The passage behind the wall was narrow enough to have an impact on her opinion of architecture. Sulfur lingered near an old heating pipe. Rust caught her sleeve. She wrapped the fragile cotton in a bandage from her bag, lest it tear further, and kept moving. Her mother had called such tunnels the underside of hospitality: the part that allowed first-class guests to believe dinner arrived by grace.","Through a grille, Mara heard Rowan ask the auctioneer to classify the chairs as fraud or incompetence. A handful of guests joined him. A majority of the room was now looking in the wrong direction.","She opened the hatch beneath the display table. The flower sat above her, mounted on marquetry that imitated a garden. A mouse could have made the journey more elegantly. Mara was satisfied with merely completing it.","Her razor slid between the frame and the panel. There: a set of wires, a silicon tag, and an analogue counter. This was not the original mechanism. The flower had become a container.","She did not cut the wires. In restoration, as in theft, the needlessness of a dramatic gesture was often a reason to avoid it. Instead, she pressed the hidden nut her mother had designed. The silver petals opened, gentle as a hand waking from sleep.","A folded invoice dropped into Mara's palm.","It named an unnamed patient, accommodation charges, and a transfer duration of thirteen months. At its foot someone had written: Witness retains sequence. Do not isolate without authorization.","Mara stopped breathing. Beneath the line was a small drawing of the flower.","\"You should get started,\" a man's voice said above her. \"The glass will rise in a minute.\"","She looked through the narrow gap. A dark-haired stranger stood at the display, one hand resting beside the dome. His manner was calm. A pale scar crossed the back of his wrist. Behind him, the auctioneer was trying to cease Rowan's furniture audit.","\"Then prevent it,\" Mara whispered.","\"That would invalidate my invitation.\"","\"Imagine my agony.\"","His mouth moved, nearly a smile. \"I can visualize it.\"","A click sounded. The counter began to accelerate. Mara pressed the silver flower against her blouse and reached for the hatch. Her heel caught the panel. For one terrible second, she could neither stand nor retreat.","The stranger moved the table a quarter of an inch. It was enough. She emerged into the glitter of the room carrying stolen property against her heart.","A security officer turned.","\"Miss,\" he said. \"What is that?\"","Mara could conceive a broad range of answers. None would survive an inspection by the city's Inspectional Services Department, whose regulation on cultural goods she had violated substantially. She was considering the possibility of a leap through a window when the stranger took off his coat and held it open.","\"You found her,\" he said to the officer, with the relieved air of a man reunited with his sole remaining good decision. \"I thought my fiancée had abandoned me.\"","The room became quiet in a collective, hungry way.","Mara stared at him. He leaned close enough for his next words to remain hers alone.","\"Cassian Vey. You may refuse. If you accept, take my arm. I can get us outside.\"","She knew the name. Everyone did. The ceremonial lord who had rescued people from a research fire, then been accused of causing it. A detective with a title, a damaged reputation, and an absence in his own memory. The gossip around him had developed a malignant life of its own.","\"You're late,\" she said, and took his arm. \"Again.\"","Rowan made an unbelieving noise. Then he began to clap, a talented cheerleader for an arrangement he plainly did not understand.","\"Is this a proper engagement?\" a woman asked.","\"That depends on your definition,\" Mara said.","Cassian's fingers remained loose against hers. \"We shall provide a more intelligible statement after dinner.\"","The officer frowned. \"The flower—\"","\"Was listed for private examination,\" Cassian said. \"Cross-check the catalogue. My registration is on the table.\"","It was. Mara saw his card beneath the raised dome. His contribution to the evening had been considered well before hers.","They passed an extravagant portrait of a pope, a tapestry called The New World, and a progressive installation whose immersive soundscape resembled a walker dragging cutlery across stone. A passerby offered congratulations. Cassian accepted with the grim courtesy of a man enduring a shower of small knives.","At the service door Mara whispered, \"You cannot marry a woman whose name you don't know.\"","\"I know your name.\"","\"That makes this substantially worse.\"","\"Mara Vale. Applicant for three restoration scholarships. Refused one because the donor required exclusive ownership of your work.\"","\"Your research needs to contain fewer personal details.\"","\"And yours needs to contain a way out.\"","She glanced at the locked door. \"I have a way out.\"","\"Of course.\"","He stepped aside. He did not insist on being the arbiter of her competence. For that alone, she almost forgave him. She opened the lock in four seconds, using the pencil's metal sleeve and a pin from her hair.","Outside, cold air washed the perfume from her throat. A man smoking a cigarette beside a van looked up. Cassian shifted without touching her, placing himself between Mara and the stranger's gaze.","\"Do you know him?\" she asked.","\"No. That is why I pay attention to him.\"","His car waited beyond the kitchen bins. She stopped before its open door.","\"A pact,\" she said. \"You don't get the flower merely because you rescued me. You don't touch me for an audience without asking. And the engagement ends when I choose.\"","\"Agreed.\"","\"Do not agree with such speed. It makes me resent you.\"","\"Would a fruitless struggle improve the experience?\"","She almost smiled. \"A little.\"","He gave her the coat. \"Then I must insist you wear this. Your hand is bleeding.\"","Only now did she notice the injury. The silver petals had cut her palm. She drew the invoice farther into her sleeve.","Cassian saw the movement and looked away. That discretion mattered more than his title.","Inside the car, his phone began to boot up an encrypted screen. Its first message came from an unknown sender: Do not let her keep the original.","Mara held the flower tighter.","\"In general,\" she said, \"do your evenings end in threats?\"","\"Oftentimes.\"","\"What an eligible bachelor.\"","He closed the car door with a care that did not resemble ownership. \"You haven't heard the worst aspect yet.\"","\"There is worse?\"","\"My aunt will expect us for dinner.\"","For the first time that night, Mara considered returning to the police."],"questions":[{"type":"inference","stem":"What does Cassian’s private offer class=\"question\" id=\"q-1-1\" data-question=\"0,0\"><p class=\"meta\">1. inference</p><p><strong>What does Cassian’s private offer during the auction chiefly allow Mara to do?</strong></p><div class=\"options\"><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"0\">A. She put her tea down too abruptly and caught the saucer before it could fall.","He pretended not to notice. Not a diagnosis.\"","Cassian slid across the file. \"Five years ago, I interviewed a railway porter called Tomas Reed. He had seen equipment moved before the fire. Ask him about the game.","Mara thought of her mother. Grief made unpredictable connections; a familiar slant of a letter could send her in one wave after another through years she had already survived. This was not Elena's handwriting. She knew that for sure. No public declaration of family obligation. None of these developments involved Mara. A society writer described her as an out-of-town beauty with ancestral connections to a family that had been extinct for two generations.","\"Impressive independence,\" June Bell said. \"You have married a dead man's family before breakfast.\"","The journalist sat on Cassian's desk, eating a banana. She had arrived carrying a cab receipt and a quilt because, she said, she might have to await a response for a lifetime. Cassian had known her since she exposed a costly modification to the city's bridge contracts. Her profession had made her useful, unpopular, and ready to ask inappropriate questions.","\"How long have you loved him?\" she asked Mara.","\"Since he offered me an escape route.\"","\"A practical foundation for romance.\"","\"No romance,\" Cassian said.","June gave him a perceptive look. \"Then put the wedding aside. We have a source to find.\"","She laid a brochure on the desk. Kingfisher House advertised colorful rooms, gardening, and a programme intended to bolster independence after injury. The cover picture showed a couple of smiling residents beneath a tree. A panda toy sat on a garden stool.","Noor studied the picture. \"I recognise one resident. He consented to participation in a rehabilitation study. He should have been discharged eighteen months ago.\"","\"A relative might have asked him to stay,\" Cassian said.","\"Possibly. I don't know. That is why I want access to the original account.\"","Mara turned the brochure over. The paper smelled faintly of clove. Her mother had kept cloves in a cloth bag with old tools, partly to discourage insects and partly because she liked the scent. Mara put that thought aside before grief could convert coincidence into certainty.","Ivo Quinn arrived next, holding a disc drive like an offended infant. \"If anyone asks me to repair another decorative lamp before telling me what is wrong with the engine that powers the whole building, I will retire.\"","\"You said that last workday,\" Cassian observed.","\"I seek to become consistent.\"","Ivo had repaired the cab June had taken. He had followed her upstairs to collect payment, found the study full of anxious people, and correctly assumed somebody had broken something. Now he inspected the flower without coming in contact with the moving petals.","\"An ingenious device,\" he said. \"But functional engineering doesn't explain the extra weight. There is a layer under the base.\"","Mara felt a brief, involuntary pulse of jealousy. Then she handed him her blade. \"Show me.\"","He asked her to hold the light while he loosened two screws. Beneath a scrap of silk was a narrow strip of magnetic material.","\"A breakthrough?\" June asked.","\"A strip,\" Ivo replied. \"Its importance may crystallize later. Please don't add a trumpet to my working conditions.\"","He set the drive beside it. The old machine gave a murmur, then a click. A tiny speaker released three seconds of an instrumentalist practising scales. After that came a man's voice.","\"The eagle takes the north slope. Tell him the match was not a piece of cake.\"","A second voice began to speak, but the recording disintegrated into static.","Mara watched Cassian. His hand had gone still against the desk. His consciousness seemed entirely present; his face, however, had lost its ordinary grace.","\"The interview,\" he said. \"Reed used that phrase. He was talking about a game of chess.\"","Noor moved no closer than necessary. \"Does it remind you of anything else?\"","\"A violet jacket. A room with no window. I'm not sure whether those belong together.\"","\"Then we won't assume they do.\"","Mara had been apprehensive about the doctor taking charge of him. Instead, Noor protected the uncertainty itself, as though an unknown answer deserved kindness before anyone knew whose case it might help.","June stopped recording. \"I can publish nothing about this.\"","\"You can help us compare the voices,\" Cassian said. \"Off-line.\"","\"Above all, no cloud transcription,\" Ivo added. \"The device's age may elude a casual search, but the file won't.\"","They agreed to carry out a quiet inquiry. June would seek records of Kingfisher's ownership; Noor would contact the resident's nominated advocate. Cassian and Mara would visit the building with an ordinary request to meet its manager. Ivo would remain with the strip. Mara disliked leaving it, but a third party had to examine the material if they wanted anything more reliable than a shared assumption.","Before they left, Cassian offered her a jacket. It was plain, warm, and a little too large.","\"You could alter it,\" he said.","\"I could also steal one that fits.\"","\"That would set back our attempt at respectability.\"","\"We have an attempt?\"","\"A modest one.\"","The jacket smelled of rain rather than perfume. She wore it.","Kingfisher House stood beside a closed furniture shop. Moss covered the wall, and a fragrant soup steamed beyond an open kitchen window. Its interior looked relatively comfortable. Nothing in its appearance guaranteed kindness, but neither did comfort constitute evidence of harm.","A saleswoman in the neighbouring shop remembered deliveries at dawn. \"Medical people,\" she said. \"Or at least, they dressed to portray medical people.\"","\"Did you see their faces?\" Mara asked.","\"I saw their van blocking my door. I was overwhelmingly interested in that.\"","Cassian thanked her with such ordinary courtesy that Mara almost forgot how easily he could make himself the center of a room. Here, he chose to become unremarkable.","At reception, Cassian called Noor and obtained permission to record their discussion before placing the phone on speaker. The receptionist's shirt carried a small embroidered flame. She asked them to abide by the visitors' policy and await the manager. Mara noticed that the door behind the desk had no handle on the residents' side.","\"Fire safety?\" she asked.","\"A precaution. Some residents become confused.\"","\"Who authorizes it?\"","The woman's smile became burdensome to maintain. \"The manager can explain.\"","A small man in a gorgeous silk tie appeared. His name was Mr. Dacre, and his speech had the smooth surface of a pebble polished by years of use. Cassian introduced himself without the title. Dacre recognised him anyway.","\"We offer compassionate care,\" Dacre said. \"Our aim is empowerment.\"","\"Then an independent advocate should have no difficulty visiting,\" Noor said from Mara's phone.","Dacre looked down at the screen. \"You recorded this?\"","\"You were informed at reception,\" Cassian replied. \"Your colleague gave permission.\"","The receptionist went pale.","Mara turned slightly towards her, blocking Dacre's pointed look. \"What would an ordinary visitor be expected to do?\"","\"Provide a name.\"","\"Tomas Reed.\"","\"No resident of that name.\"","The answer came too quickly. It might have been correct; speed alone proved nothing. Mara tried another method.","\"We have a question about a chess game. The eagle takes the north slope.\"","Somewhere beyond the door, a cup fell.","Dacre moved to the handle. Cassian did not prevent him, but his stance changed. \"If anyone is hurt, your staff should check.\"","\"Everything is orderly here.\"","\"Then let them check.\"","A young attendant slipped past. Through the opening Mara saw a man crouched beside a broken cup. His hand shook as he gathered the pieces. On his wrist was a red mark the shape of an adjustable band.","He looked up and saw Cassian.","\"I didn't lose,\" he said. \"They made me retire the king.\"","Dacre closed the door.","Mara felt an ache beneath her ribs. She wanted to force her way through. Cassian's voice, low beside her, reminded her why they had come.","\"We need his name and his consent. If we provoke a transfer now, we may lose him.\"","She hated that he was right.","Dacre demanded that they leave. Cassian asked for written confirmation of the refusal. While the manager dictated a statement, Mara moved towards the display cupboard. A recipe book lay open beside a bowl of rice. Under its corner was a page printed with appointment times.","She did not take it. She photographed the visible margin, then bent to pick up a dropped pen. The man behind the door began to sing, barely loud enough to hear.","\"A little bird upon the sill, a little bird that won't keep still.\"","Mara knew the tune. It belonged to an old city play about an exile who refused to surrender his name. June, an enthusiast for playgoing, had mentioned it on the drive.","Outside, rain struck the pavement so hard that the world seemed to zoom inward around them. Mara pressed her palms against the cold wall.","\"We should have done more.\"","\"We will do more,\" Cassian said. \"But we cannot make him our scapegoat because we want a decisive ending.\"","She looked at him then. The girl was now twenty-three and working at a theatre. She could identify her father. She might be willing to help.","Mara reread the message. It was not a rescue. It was a way to begin one.","She handed Cassian the phone. His shoulder brushed hers as he read.
